[2026-10-08 13:10] PREUAT2-1 defect-follower #3 (restore readiness after the VIEW intent) — implementer fix
  Defect (live-proven on the S25+ this hour): the credential card flow worked end-to-end — the owner typed the secret on the masked card twice, the approval carried ([credential-redacted] everywhere, the redaction law proven live), the restore fired with the normalized URL — but the injection "execute: type ... -> FAILED" BOTH times. Root cause: the fixed 1000ms Config.restoreSettleMs expired before Chrome was actually front+interactive on Samsung OneUI (the activity resume + VIEW-intent page restore take longer), so the focus-first tap landed wrong, the gesture failed, and the subsequent observation read the wrong window — the run starved to REFUSED.
  Fix (robust readiness instead of a fixed guess): after restoreBrowserForeground fires the VIEW intent the loop now waits for OBSERVATION READINESS before the step continues — waitForBrowserReadiness (a loop-local suspend beside the restore seam, using the loop's own observer/usable/notePageUrl/line/clock) re-observes on the existing unusable-tree cadence until a read passes the loop's own usable-tree bar, bounded by the new Config.restoreReadinessMs (default 10s). On budget expiry the step proceeds anyway (the next observe's own starvation machinery owns a still-dark page — no new failure mode). Outcome logged loudly ("restore: browser ready after Nms" / "restore: browser not readable within budget - continuing"). The wait rides BOTH card paths (gate card + completion card) because it lives inside restoreBrowserForeground after the settle; the loud no-URL path (no intent fired) waits for nothing; overlay/never-fronting/trusted/task-ending exclusions byte-identical. restoreSettleMs stays as the pre-observe initial settle, default raised 1000 -> 2000 (dispatch-sanctioned). The readiness probes are wall-clock WITHIN the same step (the restoreSettleMs precedent): no budget consumed, no steps consumed, and the step's enumerate/decide/execute state is unchanged.
  Vision Docs Checked: 04 §2 (the restore seam is the 2026-10-08 defect-fix machinery; step order untouched — the wait sits inside the existing restore seam, before EXECUTE / before the loop continues), 03 §5 (the readiness bar IS the loop's existing usable-tree test; expiry falls through to the graceful-refusal machinery, never a new terminal), ART-5 (both numbers are Config fields), ART-2 (no engine consulted — pure observation code)
  Deviations: the ~10s budget is enforced as a re-read count (restoreReadinessMs / unusableRetryDelayMs, ~6 retries + the initial probe) rather than a clock check — the fakeable static test Clock would make a clock-only bound hang JVM tests; the count bound is deterministic in tests and ~wall-clock-identical in production. restoreSettleMs default 1000 -> 2000 (offered by the dispatch; judged helpful).
  Scope guards: $0; no site names; no vision edits; no commits; TaskGate/gate logging/exit law untouched.
  Changed files: app/src/main/kotlin/navigator/app/loop/NavigationLoop.kt + app/src/test/kotlin/navigator/app/loop/CardForegroundRestoreTest.kt (+3 tests, 18 -> 21) + app/AGENTS.md (restore-seam one-liner + verification line) + .state/AGENTS.md (index) + .state/preUAT2-1-follower3.json.

Non-credential builds byte-identical (empty-secrets default; Tier A untouched and green).

[2026-10-08 23:30] PREUAT2-4-STATEMASK2 (URL-collision deadlock, live-proven S25+ this hour: nine card approvals, nine deadlocks) — implementer fix
  Defect (live-proven): the owner's card-typed secret was a SUBSTRING of the page URL (http://127.0.0.1:8099/login.html — e.g. "login"), and every post-approval state build threw "app-authored section (URL) would carry a credential-secret value". Structural insight: the builder only knows SANCTIONED (card-typed) secrets, so the app-authored throw could only ever fire on sanctioned values — i.e. it could only deadlock the run on collisions (URL/goal/title substrings) and never catch a genuine leak (an unsanctioned secret is by construction unknown to the builder). Dead weight that kills; masking is the lawful response everywhere.
  Fix: StateTextBuilder masks every occurrence of a known secret in ALL sections — the app-authored lines (GOAL/URL/TITLE/PLAN/TASK PROGRESS/HISTORY) now fold through the same mask() as the untrusted block (doc 05 §2's render; section semantics survive — the URL line still shows the address with the colliding substring redacted); the app-authored IllegalStateException deleted; the emission-boundary invariant UNCHANGED and loud (a secret equal to a structural constant — untrusted marker, bucket header — must never be emittable). Loop-side: comments only — the cardSecrets seam already passes redactionKeys() into the build. Non-credential builds byte-identical (empty-secrets default; Tier A green as committed).
  Vision Docs Checked: 03 §4 ("Secrets never appear in any section" — satisfied by the masked render, the same reasoning the 2026-10-07 STATEMASK pass recorded for the untrusted block; no vision doc edited, reasoning recorded as a deviation), 05 §2 ([credential-redacted] render law), 06 §2a (the invariant keeps the absolute never-emitted guarantee for structural constants), 07 §2/§4 (no fixture edited; Tier A untouched and green — non-credential replays build with the empty default), ART-2/ART-4/ART-5 (no engine consulted; no new numbers).
  Deviations: the GOAL/HISTORY throw pins re-pinned to the masked renders (that re-pin IS the fix's purpose, per dispatch); redactionKeys() prefixes now MASK in app-authored sections too (supersedes the followup's "loud check covers prefixes" note — the run survives a prefix collision); disclosed corner: a secret colliding with a section PREFIX (e.g. "GOAL") now masks that line's prefix instead of reaching the invariant — the secret never appears (the law holds) but the one line's prefix mangles for that build.

[2026-10-11 09:20] ROUND 2.3 — THE NOUL WIRE-SHAPE CORRECTION (the deepest root cause; owner-directed probe)
  Trigger: the S25+ UAT run failed with 53 steps of confident verify-done (0.92-0.99) and nine exit checks refusing ATOMICALLY-TRUE propositions (urlHost conditions whose own PAGE IDENTITY line showed the matching address) at pTrue 0.35-0.60.   Investigation (live, ~$0.0001): (1) three proposition phrasings of a trivially-true condition → 0.45-0.54 (all hedge); (2) trivially-true vs trivially-false vs nonsense on one state → 0.15-0.16, indistinguishable — the value tracks the STATE, not the proposition; (3) the documented TypeSafe systemone surface (openrouter.ai/api/v1/systemone, same key, HTTP 200) behaves identically → not endpoint-specific; (4) the SDK-documented noul shape carries the question in `instructions` — NO `proposition` field — and with the question in `instructions` BOTH surfaces discriminate PERFECTLY: host-true 0.98-0.99, host-false 0.01, word-true 0.98-0.99, word-false 0.01.
  ROOT CAUSE: the Phase-3 reverse-engineered wire shape put the noul question in a `proposition` field the model IGNORES (unknown fields are tolerated — our own field law) while `instructions` held generic boilerplate; every noul answered the boilerplate. This single shape bug explains the entire exit-hedging disease from the 2026-10-06 UAT onward. (The Laya shadow's judge always put the proposition into its instructions slot — the shadow was right; only the cloud client was wrong.)
  Changed (Round 2.3): core/.../wire/SystemOneClient.kt (the noul branch emits the proposition as `instructions`, drops the ignored `proposition` field; PROMPT_VERSION → jev-instructions-3; the corrected shape documented in-code with the evidence path); core/.../decision/Types.kt (NoulQuestion KDoc: the proposition IS the wire instruction; the typed instructions field never rides a noul); six noul-carrying transcript fixtures corrected with provenance notes (cascade-noul-atomic-subtask, combined-multi-question, live-choice-noul-recipe, noul-goal-achieved/far/uncertain); app/.../observe/WindowTitles.kt (+stripChromePrefix — Chrome's window title carries a "Chrome: " prefix, live-observed polluting TITLE + PAGE IDENTITY; +the test pin). Evidence: .state/phase-10B-gate105/noul-vs-choice-experiment.md (experiments + resolution table).
  Vision Docs Checked: 02 §2 (the wire-client rules bullet amended: the noul question rides `instructions`, the proposition field retired, with the live-proof summary; version → 1.3.1), 04 §3 (the anchored-proposition law UNCHANGED — the proposition IS the instruction; no primitive redesign), 05 (row/export shapes unchanged).
  Sections Checked: the wire shape + the title reader only.

[2026-10-13] Post-UAT repair round 1 — the two live-battery defects (owner-ordered fix loop)
  Origin: the live-key emulator battery (`.state/uat10d-emulator-battery.md`) surfaced two real defects; the owner ordered the fix→verify→emulator loop until all 5 tests run successfully.
  Defect 1 (false completion, battery Test 4): a zero-executed-action GOAL_ACHIEVED ran the terminal answer extraction over `lastPost` — which exists only after an executed action — so the state sent to the writer was literally "title: \nurl: \n"; the writer answered "the browser state I received is blank" and that answer OVERWROTE the good answer the report subtask had already produced (1,929 µUSD over the live page). Fix (doc 04 §6 v1.8.1): `lastObserved` (set at every OBSERVE) is the freshest-state fallback for the terminal extraction, the informed re-plan's context and `partialResult`; the terminal call is SKIPPED when an answer already exists (exactly ONE extraction per run — the report subtask's answer is authoritative).
  Defect 2 (livelock, battery Test 5): an engine insisting on verify-done on a page-static run had every claim withheld, and a withheld claim never metered the M=2 anti-oscillation window (`noteDecision` ran only for non-finish decisions) — the window stayed armed forever, ~50 withheld claims burned ~$0.2 and the full 60-step budget without one action being tried. Fix (doc 04 §3 v1.8.1): a withheld done claim counts against the M-meter (the law's "next M decisions"); after M claims the window closes, the next claim reaches the judge (refused, ART-4), which re-arms the window and feeds StuckDetector as the law already prescribed for consecutive refused finishes.
  Tests: ExitFlowLoopTest + "each withheld done claim meters the M-window - the claim reaches the judge again" (2 judged refusals with exactly M withhelds between, 2 ExitCheckRows); LandingAndReaskLoopTest + "a zero-action achieved run answers over the step's own observation - never a blank state" (the state handed the extractor carries the step's url+title) and the report-subtask fixture re-pinned to ONE extraction (2_000 µUSD + the loud skip line). Five fixtures gained a recovery answer (`give-up`) — LandingAndReask ×4 + ConditionHonesty ×1 — they previously relied on the withheld-forever behavior; the stuck machinery now escalates as designed.
  Also: the emulator environment was prepared for a fair battery (fresh-profile consent walls dismissed once per site: allrecipes Accept All, google Reject All, BBC I agree — the walls are NOT app bugs and the honest stops stand as the recorded consent-wall limitation).
  Suite: 552 app executions / 0 failures (546 unique). `NavigationLoop.run()` measured 59,377/65,535 bytes via javap (+731 from the fix; ~6.1 KB headroom).
  Checked By: orchestrator (implementation) — verifier round requested before the emulator battery

[2026-10-13] Post-UAT repair round 2 — the battery's four further defects (owner-ordered loop, continued)
  Origin: the round-1 battery (5 live tests on emulator-5554 with the owner's key). T4 PASSED (the false completion fixed: the answer named the real BBC headline, one extraction, the loud skip line). Four defects surfaced from the other runs:
  1. **Site entry refused text-named arrivals (T1, T2).** The engine chose `open-site` DECISIVELY (p 0.59–0.82) on subtasks whose instructions name the destination ("Open the BBC News section page at bbc.com/news") while `SiteEntry.plan` accepted `urlHost` conditions only — every such step died on "open-site could not resolve a URL to open" (T2: 3× , then the card; T1: subtask 3 of the gen3 ladder). Fix (doc 04 §1 v1.8.2): a `urlContains` identity whose segment names a page (a full URL, a host[+path], or an origin-relative path resolved against the current origin) site-enters — bounded to the GOAL's or the CURRENT page's site family (a condition may only send the run where the run's own goal or its own current site already is, never to an unrelated address). REFINEMENT during the round-2 battery: the first cut required the segment to appear verbatim in the plan text; the round-2 T1 run showed the engine's decisive open-site (p 0.82 in round 1) dying on a condition that named the article exactly ('en.wikipedia.org/wiki/World_War_II' — the instruction says "Open the World War II article on English Wikipedia", not the URL string), so the text-naming guard was replaced by the destination-family bound. Residual recorded (doc 04 §1): a condition that is a proxy for an unstated outcome ("the address contains 'google.com/search'" standing for "the results are showing") can now be navigated to — ART-4's anchored noul is the backstop (live-proven discriminating: LOW 0.11–0.20 on the battery's false claims); revisit with battery evidence.
  2. **No way to submit a form whose affordance is the keyboard (T3).** The Google run typed "mushroom recipes" (stage-2 target 4.6, dispatched) and then re-typed it 3× until the stuck kill: the page shows no submit control (mobile Google submits on the IME Search key) and the `submit` intent's only execution was tapping a submit-shaped button. Fix (doc 04 §2 v1.8.2): `submit` fires the platform IME action (ACTION_IME_ENTER on the focused editable) when no submit-shaped control exists in the buttons slate — the field must be one THIS LOOP filled, and a gate-classified submission NEVER fires through the IME path (doc 06 §1: it falls back to the ordinary path where the card governs). `ActionExecutor` gains `submit()`.
  3. **The engine could not know a field was filled-but-unsubmitted (T3).** The stage-1 input (the INTENT STATE VIEW) carried the page but not the one fact the next move needs. Fix (doc 03 §4 v2.6.0): a conditional code-authored `TYPED FIELD: "<label>" holds text this run typed (not yet submitted)` line between PAGE and the excerpt, present exactly while a loop-filled field is still on the page.
  4. **Page noise could stall the escalation at L1 forever (T5).** The allrecipes page's rotating ad changed the hash between refusals, `StuckDetector.record` read "the screen moved" and reset the escalation counter every cycle — the `exit-conflict-3x` signal tripped repeatedly at LEVEL 1 for ~50 steps until the $0.25 spend cap (L2 arrived at t+502 s; the pre-round-1 silent livelock is already fixed by the round-1 meter). Fix (doc 04 §4 v1.8.2): the escalation reset requires PROGRESS — an executed action whose step also moved the screen; a refused done claim is never progress.
  Tests: SiteEntry plan pins (text-named urlContains plans; condition-only, unnamed and phrase segments never plan), a loop pin (both arrivals code-navigate, GOAL_ACHIEVED), the submit pin (triage → fill-field → submit with no submit control: the executor's `submit()` fires, the row is SUBMIT/EXECUTED, the firing is loud), the StageViews pin (the TYPED FIELD line rides exactly when given), two StuckDetector pins (refusal footprints with a moving hash still escalate; a real action + screen move still resets).
  Suite: 557 app executions / 0 failures (551 unique); 379 core / 0 failures (the Tier-A replay untouched — the stage-view text is not the recorded state). `run()` measured 61,584/65,535 bytes (+2,207 from round 2; ~3.9 KB headroom — the next loop change should mind this).
  Checked By: orchestrator (implementation) — verifier round requested before the emulator re-run

[2026-10-13] Post-UAT repair round 2 close-out — the 404 false completion + the verifier's findings
  The round-2 emulator battery's Test 5 reported GOAL_ACHIEVED on a 404 ("Page Not Found"): the writer invented '/recipes/dinner' (allrecipes' real shape differs), the site 404'd while KEEPING the requested URL, the `urlContains` guard read TRUE by substring, and the engine hedged 0.65–0.73 (Path B accepted). The last remaining false-completion class in the battery; fixed:
  - **The landing coherence (doc 04 §1 v1.8.2)**: a `urlContains` segment carrying a PATH names a specific page, so the completion guard (`DoneConditionChecker.satisfied`) additionally requires the landed page's TITLE to share at least one token with that path's LAST slug — a site's Not-Found page keeps the URL but not the title. A blank title is no evidence (coheres); a bare-host segment has no path to check; the loop logs the incoherence loudly at the landing. LIVE PROOF: the re-run refused 10 incoherent landings; the writer's informed re-plan diagnosed it in words ("Leave the 404 error page and reach a working Allrecipes page…") and produced the real '/recipes/17562/dinner' shape; no false completion remains.
  - **Verifier round-2 findings closed** (the round's report: the stale-tree compile break + the gate-10_2 expectation were fixed before it returned; the rest here): the `SiteEntry.plan` KDoc rewritten to the actual destination-bound law; the STRICT host family for the navigation bound (the reconciliation's ccTLD-brand heuristic treats `mail.google.com`/`mail.yahoo.net` as one family — fine for reconciliation, unsafe as a navigation gate); the additive-only H9 consult now gates the IME submit path too (a mutation-suspicious surface falls back so the ordinary gated path governs); the typed-field anchor RETIRES after a successful IME submit (the line can no longer claim "not yet submitted"); the stale size notes refreshed; two recommended tests added (the gate-classified IME fallback + the masked TYPED FIELD label, both in LandingAndReaskLoopTest).
  - **The residual the verifier flagged is now materially closed**: the destination bound does navigate to a condition-named URL, but a 404-shaped landing cannot complete the subtask (the coherence) and the anchored noul remains the second backstop. Recorded as an owner-visible decision in the battery record (the bound was introduced by the owner-ordered loop; the coherence was its live-proven closure).
  Suite: 559 app executions / 0 failures (553 unique); 380 core / 0 failures. `run()` measured 62,275/65,535 bytes (3,260 headroom).
  Round-2 battery: T1 GOAL_ACHIEVED then BUDGET_EXHAUSTED (wall clock) on the close-out build; T2 GOAL_ACHIEVED then BUDGET_EXHAUSTED ($0.256); T3 GOAL_ACHIEVED (the first type+submit ever executed, the answer cites `google.com/search?q=mushroom+recipes`); T4 GOAL_ACHIEVED in round 1, STUCK_KILL ×2 on writer-ladder variance; T5 the false completion eliminated (honest STUCK_KILL). The remaining failures are writer-ladder variance + engine hedging + the emulator's page-load timings, not app-law defects — detailed with next levers in `.state/uat10d-emulator-battery.md` §Round 2.
  Checked By: orchestrator (implementation) + canon-verifier (round 2; the close-out items fixed post-report and re-tested)

[2026-10-17] Hotel iteration run 2 — the ladder-path browser-readiness pre-step + the state-truthful refusal (owner picks solutions 1+4)
  Origin: run 2 ("book a hotel for next weekend in Tallinn", task-1792223659349) ended REFUSED "I can't read this site" ~20s in with ZERO decision rows and Chrome never opened (.state/hotel-iter/run2/REPORT.md). Corrected root cause: the PRODUCT path is the LADDER branch of NavigationLoop.run, which subsumes the cold-start pre-step; ColdStart.run exists only on the legacy non-ladder branch — so no code brought a browser to the front, the loop's step-0 observe read the app's own setup screen, the readiness bar failed, and the graceful refusal misattributed the state. Owner picked solutions 1 (readiness fallback) + 4 (truthful refusal + one-tap recovery) from the report; MANDATORY design interview run (both rounds, 2026-10-17): personal owner-only app; smallest change; matches the existing warm brand-forward dark-first compact product (2026-10-09 tokens); recovery lives as an ACTION ROW in the corner live log (explicitly NOT a card, NOT a notification); tap = open the browser + AUTO RE-RUN the same goal; shown ONLY on browser-fixable refusals; no new design system. Recorded here so the UI provenance is traceable.
  Fix 1 (doc 04 §1 v1.8.3): the ladder-path browser-readiness pre-step — code-decided, engine-free (ART-2), READINESS NOT NAVIGATION. New seam `BrowserHomeLauncher` (ColdStart.kt, beside BrowserProbe/BrowserLauncher); NavigationLoop's nullable `homeLauncher` ctor param (default null = every existing path byte-identical). The pre-step runs ONCE per run before the step loop's first observe (after the ladder is applied + enterSubtask(), mirroring the legacy cold-start's position), fires the HOME launch only when ALL hold: launcher wired + browser installed (probe) + the fresh observation's ACTIVE window is a non-blank NON-browser package; deliberately silent on blank packages, browser-already-front (mid-run tree recovery stays the starvation machinery's job), and browser-absent; then reuses the existing waitForBrowserReadiness bounded wait; probes consume no budget/steps; ONE attempt per run (`readinessAttempted`). Legacy non-ladder path untouched. Generalization law: no host/site/label/language string anywhere — "the browser" is the generic noun.
  Fix 4a (doc 03 §5 v2.7.0 + doc 04 §1): the state-truthful refusal — `notePageUrl` (the single choke point every observe path flows through) now records `everBrowserFront` + `lastFrontWindow`; at the observeUsable-null refusal, a never-browser-front run's REFUSED detail reads "the browser never came to the front, so there was no page to read (front window: <last package or unknown>)" instead of "I can't read this site"; a browser-once-front run keeps the doc-03 message byte-identically.
  Fix 4b (the interview's UI deliverable): `LiveLogRecovery` — the PURE JVM-pinned eligibility decision (`recoveryEligible(terminal, everBrowserFront)` = REFUSED && !everBrowserFront; label exactly "Open the browser and retry") beside the overlay's other pure model. LiveLogOverlay renders ONE compact amber action row under the terminal state line (the controlButton pill language, the brand-accent token; reuses the window's existing touch handling); the loop reports the facts fire-and-forget via the ADDITIVE default-noop `LoopUiListener.onRecoveryOffer` (ART-2 intact — the decision is computed at the overlay edge from the facts); the row disappears on the tap, a new run (show() resets), the log's ✕ close, and service teardown, and never reappears for a non-eligible terminal. NavigatorService wires `onRecoveryAction`: tap = dismiss + `openBrowserHome()` (the platform launch intent for the allowlisted browser, derived by the SAME HarnessObserver.installedBrowser the probe uses; wired null when none resolves) + re-run the SAME goal through handleGoalSubmitted (the exact goal-input channel; `lastGoalText` lives in memory only — ART-8 holds, goals are not secrets).
  Method-size: the first cut broke the JVM 64KB cap (MethodTooLargeException on NavigationLoop.run — the documented hazard); fixed by the Phase-10D lesson: the three new per-run fields (`everBrowserFront`/`lastFrontWindow`/`readinessAttempted`) moved to CLASS fields (reset at run() start) and the refusal-detail string building to a class-scope `observeRefusalDetail()` helper. run() re-measured via javap: 63,330/65,535 (~2.2 KB headroom; was 62,275).
  Tests: `LadderReadinessPrestepTest` (7 — the fired-once-then-proceeds run incl. the exact readiness line; browser-front never fires; launcher-null never fires; probe-null never fires; blank windowPackage never fires; the never-front refusal names the truth + the recovery facts; the browser-once-front refusal keeps "I can't read this site") + `LiveLogRecoveryTest` (2 — the eligibility matrix over every terminal; the exact label). Suite: :core 380 / 0 failures (full rerun); :app 571 / 0 failures (562 prior + 9 new); :app:assembleDebug green.
  Canon: doc 04 → v1.8.3 (§1 pre-step law + truthful-refusal law); doc 03 → v2.7.0 (§5 variant note). No prompt/wire/fixture change (no GenClient surface touched); no row/schema change (TerminalRow.detail is free text); golden fixtures untouched.
  Checked By: orchestrator (implementation) — canon-verifier round requested before the owner's run-3

## 2026-10-18 — PHASE_VEXP1 post-verification defect fix: the escalation-without-action guard
* Checker: canon-implementer (owner's emulator test surfaced the defect after the Verifier PASS); verifier re-check of the fix next
* Evidence: emulator run vision-loop-1792356890231 ("find a hotel in Riga for next weekend", booking.com webview shell) — step 0 tapped the cookie Decline via Jev Box (iou 0.61 conf 0.95); steps 2/3/4 BATTERY ESCALATE on an UNCHANGED screen (screenshots #6/#7 sha256-identical, no action between escalations); the bad-is-true check ("does the date field still show the old default dates?") is true BY DEFINITION until an action runs; re-plan #2 wrote a correct plan that never received a step; REPLANS_EXHAUSTED in 5 steps / 2 re-plans / zero actions after step 0. Root cause: the battery escalation path skips attemptStep, so the frozen screen re-fires the identical battery verdict and starves the loop of actions.
* Fix (VisionLoop only): class field `batteryEscalatedWithoutAction` — reset per run; set at the re-plan gate ONLY when the same iteration's escalation came from the battery (stepEscalation non-null); the next iteration SKIPS the battery (loud "battery held one step" log + evidence event) and runs attemptStep normally; ANY action attempt (acted or failed) clears it and the battery re-arms from the following step; escalations still consume the existing re-plan budget (no new budget); batteryGate/max-gate/thresholds/typed escalation/evidence lines untouched.
* Test seam (recorded deviation, outside the VisionLoop-only fix): VisionCapture's constructor param made nullable (requireNotNull at the single use site) so the JVM loop test constructs it with null and rides the loop's loud text-only degrade; production wiring unchanged.
* Test: new scripted loop test (VisionExperimentTest) — full VisionLoop.run over ScriptedVisionTransport/ScriptedObserver/RecordingExecutor/VisionCapture(null): escalate -> re-plan -> battery HELD -> tap -> battery re-arms -> second escalation -> REPLANS_EXHAUSTED at maxReplans=1; asserts 1 tap, 2 jev battery calls (the held step fired none), 3 writer calls, stepsUsed 3, and the action line strictly between the two escalation lines.
* Gates re-run after the fix: :app vision tests 34/34; :app:testDebugUnitTest 623/0; :core:test BUILD SUCCESSFUL (app-only fix, inputs unchanged); :app:assembleDebug BUILD SUCCESSFUL; aapt2 badging navigator.app.visionexp / 0.1.0-visionexp; dex marker vision-exp-v6 re-verified (classes13.dex). No device install, no commit.

## 2026-10-18 — PHASE_VEXP1 defect-fix round 2: the accept-side battery gate (false-completion repair)
* Checker: canon-implementer (owner's live re-run surfaced the defect after the round-1 fix); verifier re-check of both rounds next
* Evidence: emulator run vision-loop-1792358155819 ("find a hotel in Riga for next weekend") — step 0 Jev-box-tapped the check-in date field (IoU 0.82, picker opened); step 2 tapped "Search properties" via haiku-box-fallback at LOW-CONF (IoU 0.12) which did NOT run the search; terminal GOAL_ACHIEVED with replans=0; the final screenshot shows the date picker OPEN, default dates Sun Oct 18 -> Mon Oct 19, NO results list. Root cause: the FINAL subtask's done-condition text matched the page's own hero heading ("Search hotels in Riga" is literally printed on the page) — a markerText-style mention-match accepted a false completion on the ACCEPT path, where the verification battery is NOT consulted (it only escalates pre-act).
* Fix 1 — ACCEPT-SIDE BATTERY GATE (VisionLoop): before ANY accept, if the ACTIVE subtask's battery carries outcome checks, the battery must CONCUR — new pure `VisionLoop.batteryConcurs` (max-gate red flags refuse; every outcome check achieved via the same two-channel rule as batteryGate — flagHigh/achieved extracted as shared companion helpers, batteryGate semantics byte-identical; a battery without outcome checks cannot speak); reuse-or-run: the accept gate and the pre-act battery share a screen-hash-keyed results cache (`lastBatteryResults`/`lastBatteryHash`, cleared on subtask advance + re-plan + per run) so a refused accept on an unchanged screen never pays for a second identical battery; a refusal is loud ("step N: ACCEPT REFUSED - the code condition is satisfied but the verify battery does not concur (the outcome is not visible)"), advances nothing, consumes its step, feeds consecutiveFailures -> the existing re-plan budget; the loop then falls through to the ordinary stuck-probe/pre-act/attemptStep machinery (it keeps acting). Code = condition satisfied; battery = outcome visible; BOTH must concur.
* Fix 2 — PLANNER LAW (VisionPrompts): PLAN_SYSTEM now requires the FINAL subtask to ALWAYS carry a verify battery over the VISIBLE end state (a results list, a confirmation screen, the cart contents — never a heading or hero text that exists on every page of the site; a badIsTrue check welcome). VISION_PROMPT_VERSION bumped vision-exp-v6 -> vision-exp-v7 per the file's own bump law (prompt text changed); the phase-doc gate's "v6 dex marker" describes the pre-fix build — the rebuilt APK carries v7.
* Tests (+4 = vision 34 -> 38): the scripted false-completion loop test (hero-text condition + failing outcome check -> ACCEPT REFUSED, no GOAL_ACHIEVED, REPLANS_EXHAUSTED at maxReplans=1, 1 tap); the concurrence loop test (outcome check true -> GOAL_ACHIEVED on the visible end state, replans 0); the concurrence matrix (red flag refuses / unachieved refuses / both-channels-refused refuses / image-confirmed alone concurs / no outcome checks -> code rules alone); the PLAN_SYSTEM final-subtask law pin + version v7. The round-1 loop-guard test unchanged and green.
* Gates re-run: :app vision tests 38/38; :app:testDebugUnitTest 627/0 (full); :core:test 380/0 (fresh rerun — app-only fix); :app:assembleDebug BUILD SUCCESSFUL; aapt2 badging navigator.app.visionexp / 0.1.0-visionexp; dex marker vision-exp-v7 present (classes13.dex). No device install, no commit.

## 2026-10-18 — PHASE_VEXP1 defect-fix round 3: the battery re-plan budget split (budget coalescing)
* Checker: canon-implementer (owner's live re-run after round 2); verifier re-check of rounds 1-3 next
* Live result first: the loop now ACTS and the battery STEERS — Jev-box taps landed (date field IoU 0.73, then the "23" calendar cell IoU 0.51) and the round-1 hold-guard worked. New bottleneck: BUDGET COALESCING — the battery fired OUTCOME-ACHIEVED-WHILE-CONDITION-UNMET at step 4 (check-in selected; the done-condition still pointed at the unset state — a genuinely mis-shaped condition whose re-plan would have said "check-in done, now select check-out"), but maxReplans (2) was already consumed by two earlier keep-acting red-flag escalations -> REPLANS_EXHAUSTED mid-progress.
* Fix (VisionLoop only): the battery-driven re-plan budget SPLIT — new Config field `batteryReplansPerSubtask = 3` + the `batteryReplans` counter. Gate order in the battery-escalate path: batteryReplans < budget -> re-plan under the battery budget (counter++, outcomes line "REPLAN (battery #N): ..."); else -> NO re-plan, NO step burned — forced PROCEED (outcomes/log/evidence line "battery budget exhausted for this subtask - acting per cascade") and attemptStep runs (the round-1 act-first guard already keeps the battery from re-firing before an action after any re-plan). The re-plan gate's budget follows the ENTRY (a `batteryReplanQueued` flag set at the escalate site): stuck/step-failure entries still spend maxReplans (2) per task. Resets: subtask advance and failure-driven re-plan success (the new plan gets fresh battery budget); a battery-driven re-plan does NOT reset its own counter — that would make the forced-PROCEED arm unreachable, contradicting the mandated test (interpretation recorded in the status file). Max-gate semantics, thresholds, and existing evidence lines unchanged.
* Tests (vision 38 -> 39): the round-1 loop-guard test rewritten for the split semantics (first escalation re-plans under the battery budget; the spent budget forces PROCEED; termination moves to the failure budget) + the NEW two-subtask scripted test: escalate -> battery re-plan x2 -> spent-budget escalation forces PROCEED + attemptStep (NO re-plan chat) -> accept advances the subtask (budget resets) -> the next subtask's first escalation re-plans again -> GOAL_ACHIEVED; asserts 4 taps, exactly 1 budget-exhausted line, exactly 3 "REPLAN (battery" lines, replans == 0 (failure budget untouched).
* Gates re-run: :app vision tests 39/39; :app:testDebugUnitTest 628/0 (full); :core:test BUILD SUCCESSFUL (app-only fix; core inputs unchanged — 380/0 fresh rerun after round 2 stands); :app:assembleDebug BUILD SUCCESSFUL; aapt2 badging navigator.app.visionexp / 0.1.0-visionexp; dex marker vision-exp-v7 present (classes13.dex — no prompt change in round 3, version stays v7). No device install, no commit.

## 2026-10-18 — PHASE_VEXP5 ORCHESTRATOR REVIEW AMENDMENT: THE NO-RAW-TOUCH INVARIANT CLOSED (implementer self-run gates; NOT verification)
* Checker: canon-implementer (canon loop, exp/vision-box); cold verification owned by the canon-verifier
* Review findings (orchestrator): (1) the STUCK PROBE's `executor.scroll(obs.nodes.lastOrNull()?.centerX ?: 540, 1200, down = true)` was an unguarded raw touch that could land on the live log; (2) `VisionMath.uncoveredPoint` fell back to the centre when every candidate was covered, which could dispatch a raw scroll onto the overlay.
* Changed: `VisionMath.uncoveredPoint` is now `Pair<Int, Int>?` and returns NULL when every candidate is covered (the best-effort centre fallback is REMOVED — the stricter law). `VisionLoop` gained the private shared `scrollStart(occluders, shot, x, y)` helper used by BOTH the ACT SCROLL branch and the stuck probe: an uncovered start (or null coveredBounds) returns the intended point (dispatch byte-identical); a covered start moves to the first uncovered candidate inside the captured frame; NULL (all covered, or no frame) = NO `executor.scroll` at all. The ACT branch fails loudly with `scroll start (x,y) is covered by the live log or keyboard and no uncovered point exists inside the captured frame; refusing a touch that would land on the overlay`; the stuck probe logs + evidences `stuck probe skipped: covered start and no uncovered point` and falls through to the existing still-static `consecutiveFailures` path. * Run 1 (`task-1792406038348`): every frame `scope=window:org.chromium.webview_shell:id=281:bounds=0,0,1080,2340` (723x1568); step 0 `[node n8 iou=0.94 (jev-box) conf=0.95] (behind-log: node click)`, evidence `covered action: tap (740,1979) -> node click`; the keyboard was the occluder (log top-right), the node click worked, the page became TAPPED.
* Run 2 (`task-1792406239937`, IME disabled so the log stayed bottom-right over the button): every frame `scope=window:org.chromium.webview_shell:id=308:bounds=0,0,1080,2340`; step 0 `[node n8 iou=0.84 (jev-box) conf=0.93] (behind-log: node click)`, same `covered action: tap (740,1979) -> node click`; page TAPPED. * Caveats: the fixture's URL-shaped done condition did not accept the page's TAPPED marker (the battery flagged the mis-shaped condition and re-planned; the runs were ended after the step-0 evidence — a fixture/planner mismatch, not a VEXP5 failure); a direct SetupActivity submit hit the known emulator readiness home-launch (getRunningTasks restricted → about:blank), so the fixture runs raised the goal overlay via the running service `ACTION_SHOW_GOAL_INPUT` to preserve the browser task; the debug app was force-stopped after the evidence and the a11y service was re-enabled/relaunched (the product app `navigator.app` was untouched); the first-frame `[vision] screen:` line duplicates on the first run after a service start (cosmetic reset order).

## 2026-10-20 — PHASE 15 POST-VERIFICATION LAUNCH-CRASH REPAIR (live device)
* Finding: after the owner uninstalled/reinstalled the app, Setup crashed on launch with `java.lang.RuntimeException: java.lang.reflect.InvocationTargetException` → `javax.crypto.AEADBadTagException` in `EncryptedSharedPreferences.create` at `OpenRouterKeyStore.prefs` (called from `SetupActivity.kt:190` in composition). The Android auto-backup restored `navigator-secure-prefs.xml` on reinstall, but the Keystore master key was destroyed with the uninstall, so the restored keyset could not decrypt.
* Fix: `OpenRouterKeyStore` self-heals a corrupt/unreadable keyset — it deletes the encrypted prefs file and the default `_androidx_security_master_key_` alias, then recreates; `load`/`has`/`save` are exception-safe (save returns Boolean; the Setup only clears the field/refreshes on success); the manifest sets `android:allowBackup="false"` so the encrypted store is never restored without its key.
* Evidence: `:app:testDebugUnitTest` = 655/0/0; `:app:assembleDebug` SUCCESS; the fixed APK installed over the corrupt install; a clean launch log has NO `AndroidRuntime` crash; the Setup renders at 2 of 4 (accessibility + battery done, both keys missing); the corrupt `navigator-secure-prefs.xml` is gone; `ALLOW_BACKUP` is absent from the installed package. The old keys were already unrecoverable after the uninstall; the owner must re-enter both.
* No commit: the repair and the translate-first change are on `phase-15-translate`, uncommitted, pending the owner's key entry/UAT and explicit commit request.
* Security/compliance: no key values were read, printed or logged; no live model call. COMPLIANT.

## 2026-10-20 — PHASE 15 KEY-STORE SAVE FIX (live device, continuation)
* Finding: after the first crash self-heal the app launched but Save still did nothing. A plain file delete left the process's `SharedPreferences` cache holding the corrupt keyset, and the default Keystore alias stayed wedged; `save` returned false silently.
* Fix: `OpenRouterKeyStore` now uses a VERSIONED v2 prefs file (`navigator-secure-prefs-v2`). On an unreadable/restored store it clears the cached legacy + current prefs (`Context.deleteSharedPreferences`), deletes the files and the default `_androidx_security_master_key_` alias, then recreates. `load`/`has`/`save` are exception-safe; `save` returns Boolean and the Setup only clears the field/card on success. The app keeps `android:allowBackup="false"`.
* Evidence: `:app:testDebugUnitTest` = 655/0/0; `:app:assembleDebug` SUCCESS; fixed APK installed; launch log has no `AndroidRuntime` crash and no `NavigatorKeys` failure; a NON-SECRET test value saved successfully — the OpenRouter card turned Done (3 of 4), `navigator-secure-prefs-v2.xml` was written (1294 bytes), no failure log. The test value is a placeholder; the owner replaces it with the real OpenRouter key and enters the TypeSafe key, then Test keys.
* No commit: the save fix, the crash repair and the translate-first change are on `phase-15-translate`, uncommitted, pending the owner's UAT and explicit commit request.
* Security/compliance: no key value was read, printed or logged (the test value is a dummy); no live model call. COMPLIANT.

## 2026-10-20 — PHASE_VEXP9 OBJECTIVE CORRECTED (owner: efficiency, not cheapest-sufficient)

* Personal-data note added: R3/R4 goal text and page content carry the owner's address/e-mails/subscriptions; durable reports quote the outcome, not the raw data; host CSVs are deleted after each iteration report.
* Status: `.state/phase-vexp9-status.json` carries the corpus + rules. No law change; no key values; no device/live model call. COMPLIANT.

## 2026-10-20 — PHASE_VEXP9 R3 MONITOR-STOP RULE (owner: keep the goal, stop at the card screen)
* Owner correction: R3's goal text stays exactly as written; the safety is a **monitoring duty**, not a goal rewrite. The orchestrating agent watches the run and stops it when the screenshot shows a credit-card input screen.
* Contract changes: §5.1b rewritten (the stop condition, the monitoring agent as the actor, the ~5 s frame-review cadence via navcap, the "a monitor stop is not a failure" rule, the force-stop fail-safe); §5.1a R3 anchor judged at the stop point; `runs.csv` gains `reset_ok`, `stop_reason`, `stopped_at_ms`; new WI8 (the experiment-only `EXPERIMENT_STOP_TASK` broadcast in `OverlayDebugReceiver`, debuggable-only, reusing `OverlayManager.onStopTask` → `TaskCancelFlag` → USER_CANCEL); gate 9 pins the stop path. The purchase-rule open question is resolved and removed.
* Mechanism verified in code: `ACTION_STOP_TASK` exists only as the notification's `PendingIntent.getService` (`NavigatorService.kt:601-636`), so an adb stop is not wired today; `OverlayManager.onStopTask` is a public callback (`OverlayManager.kt:236`) set by the service (`NavigatorService.kt:209`) — WI8 reuses it with no new state plumbing.
* Status: `.state/phase-vexp9-status.json` updated + JSON-validated. No law change; no key values; no device/live model call. COMPLIANT.
